assert line in s; s=s.replace(line,'',1)

---
## Summary
The home page is now assembled from backend blocks. `GET /api/homepage` returns the enabled blocks of the `homepage_blocks` table in order, and the frontend has one renderer per block type (hero carousel, side tiles, platform row, product rows). Each renderer fetches its own endpoint (`/api/blocks/...`).
The content is in the database, inserted by migration `00006`, not by the seed (so no reseed is needed). A block can be switched off without a deploy (shown on `dev`).
Blocks fail independently. The carousel is accessible (pauses on hover/focus, off with reduced motion) and its first picture is the LCP (eager, `fetchpriority=high`, `srcset`); CLS is 0 on desktop and 0.001 on mobile.
Footer: Kodmo Auto link. README: "About the author".

**⚠️ This PR has a migration: run *Actions → Migrate production → Run workflow* from `task/15-homepage` BEFORE merging (check that `00006_homepage.sql` is applied). No reseed.**

# Continuous integration: checks every pull request (and every push to main).
# Three independent jobs; their names are the "required checks" of the protect-main ruleset:
#   go          - formatting, vet, build and unit tests of the Go code
#   migrations  - every migration (Up AND Down) on a clean Postgres + DB integration tests
#   frontend    - lint, unit tests and production build of the React app
name: CI

on:
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]
  push:
    branches: [main]

# A newer push to the same PR makes the older, still running run pointless: cancel it.
# Runs on main are never cancelled: every merge keeps its own complete result.
concurrency:
  group: ci-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: ${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' }}

# The jobs only read the repository.
permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:

**Tool: Write**

```
C:\Users\[USER]\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\27133936c4ab5668\60d9fad0-2c1e-4660-a781-65a98ec415b1\scratchpad\convert_log.py
---
# Converts a Claude Code session transcript (JSONL) into the Markdown session log format used in ai-logs/.
# Usage: python -I convert_log.py <transcript.jsonl> <out.md> <title> <stop-marker-text>
import json
import re
import sys

src, out, title, stop_marker = sys.argv[1:5]

RULES = [
    (re.compile(r"postgres(?:ql)?://[^\s\"'`<>]+"), "[REDACTED]"),
    (re.compile(r"npg_[A-Za-z0-9]+"), "[REDACTED]"),
    (re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9-]+\.[a-z0-9-]+\.aws\.neon\.tech[^\s\"'`]*"), "[REDACTED]"),
    (re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9-]*neon\.tech[^\s\"'`]*"), "[REDACTED]"),
    (re.compile(r"\b[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@(?!example\.com|demo\.invalid|anthropic\.com)[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}\b"), "[REDACTED]"),
    (re.compile(r"vercel.com/[REDACTED]/"), "vercel.com/[REDACTED]/"),
    (re.compile(r"(?i)(IGDB_CLIENT_(?:ID|SECRET)|DATABASE_URL(?:_DIRECT)?)=\S+"), r"\1=[REDACTED]"),
    (re.compile(r"(?i)(access_token|client_secret)[\"']?\s*[:=]\s*[\"']?[A-Za-z0-9]{12,}"), r"\1=[REDACTED]"),
    (re.compile(r"\\Users\\[USER]\b"), r"\\Users\\[USER]"),
    (re.compile(r"/Users/[USER]\b"), "/Users/[USER]"),
    (re.compile(r"\bdovyd\b"), "[USER]"),
]


def redact(text):
    for pat, rep in RULES:
        text = pat.sub(rep, text)
    return text


def short(text, limit):
    text = text.strip("\n")
    if len(text) <= limit:
        return text

    if name == "Read":
        return inp.get("file_path", "")
    return json.dumps(inp, ensure_ascii=False)

## Claude · 2026-11-23T09:48:50.702Z

The Task 15 home page is built and PR #19 is open. CI is green on the final commit (`go`, `frontend`, `migrations`, Vercel Preview).

**Before merging, you need to run *Actions → Migrate production* from `task/15-homepage`** and check that the log shows `00006_homepage.sql` applied. No reseed is needed.

- **How it works:** the server lists the blocks in order from the `homepage_blocks` table. The frontend has one renderer per block type (hero carousel, side tiles, platform row, product rows), and each block loads from its own endpoint. All the content comes from migration `00006`; the seed is unchanged.
- **Switching a block off:** I turned "Biggest discounts" off in `dev` and the row disappeared on reload with no deploy; it is back on now. In production the change will take up to about 5 minutes because of the CDN cache.
- **When something fails:** only the failing block shows "could not be loaded" with Retry. A block that crashes while drawing hides itself, and the rest of the page keeps working.
- **Checks:** all local tests, lint and build pass, including 573 frontend tests. The 6 new database tests only ran in CI, because there's no Postgres on this machine; there all 171 database tests passed with 0 skipped.
- **In the browser:** axe found 0 violations at three widths on every slide. Lighthouse desktop: LCP 1.3 s, CLS 0 (mobile: LCP 5.0 s on simulated slow 4G, CLS 0.001). The LCP is the Innert image, loaded eagerly with high priority.
- **Also done:** the Innert banners are WebP files of 49–133 KB, the footer has the Kodmo Auto link, and the README has "About the author". The task reviewer gave PASS; I fixed its one small README issue.

Things I changed from the task (all in the report):
- **Two extra image columns** for the responsive image sizes.
- **A Play/Pause button** on the carousel, which accessibility guidelines require.
- **Product slides are hidden** when the product is out of stock, so there is no "Buy now" for something you can't buy.

Questions for you (report §6):
1. The tile says "Up to −70%" but the biggest discount in the catalog is now 73%. Keep the text, change it, or show the live number?
2. "Popular right now" shows several editions of the same game (three GTA V, two Witcher 3). Should each game appear only once?
3. Should the carousel respond to swiping on phones? Right now it's dots, Play/Pause and the keyboard only.

PR: https://github.com/dovydas472/ENEBA-homework/pull/19
Report: [docs/reports/TASK_15_homepage.md](docs/reports/TASK_15_homepage.md)

c:\PROJEKTAI\ENEBA\ENEBA-homework\pkg\homepage\service.go
--- old
// distinctCovers returns
--- new
// maxDiscount is the biggest discount (whole percent) of the products that can be bought now, or 0
// when none is discounted. It is the first card of the Store's "discount" listing - the same list the
// tile links to (/store?sort=discount) - so the number on the tile is the one the shopper finds there.
func (s *Service) maxDiscount(ctx context.Context) (int64, error) {
	page, err := s.catalog.ListProducts(ctx, catalog.Filter{Sort: catalog.SortDiscount, Page: 1, PageSize: 1})
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	if len(page.Items) == 0 {
		return 0, nil
	}
	first := page.Items[0]
	// The discount sort puts out-of-stock products last, so an out-of-stock first card means none is buyable.
	if !first.InStock || first.DiscountPercent == nil {
		return 0, nil
	}
	return *first.DiscountPercent, nil
}

// distinctCovers returns

	cases := map[string][]catalog.Item{
		"empty catalog":            {},

				{ID: 1, Title: "Up to −{maxDiscount}%", Href: "/store?sort=discount", Accent: "promo"},
				{ID: 2, Title: "Steam games", Href: "/store?platform=steam", Accent: "violet"},

			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if len(got.Tiles) != 1 || got.Tiles[0].Title != "Steam games" {
				t.Errorf("tiles = %+v, want only Steam games", got.Tiles)
			}
		})
	}
}

Now the integration test: the migrated title should give the same number as the Store's discount listing, and it should follow a price change.

**Tool: Edit**

```
c:\PROJEKTAI\ENEBA\ENEBA-homework\pkg\homepage\store_integration_test.go
--- old
// The CHECK constraints and the unique index of 00006 refuse bad content, whatever the code does.
--- new
// Migration 00007: the discount tile says "Up to −N%" where N is the biggest discount of the Store's
// discount listing right now - and it follows the catalog without anyone editing the tile.
func TestHomepageIntegration_discountTileShowsTheLiveMaxDiscount(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()

	discountTile := func() string {
		t.Helper()
		var tiles homepage.Tiles
		if code := e.get(t, "/api/blocks/tiles", &tiles); code != http.StatusOK {
			t.Fatalf("tiles status %d", code)
		}
		for _, tile := range tiles.Tiles {
			if tile.Href == "/store?sort=discount" {
				return tile.Title
			}
		}
		t.Fatal("no discount tile")
		return ""
	}
	topOfDiscountListing := func() catalog.Item {
		t.Helper()
		page, err := e.catalog.ListProducts(ctx, catalog.Filter{Sort: catalog.SortDiscount, Page: 1, PageSize: 1})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		return page.Items[0]
	}

	top := topOfDiscountListing()
	if top.DiscountPercent == nil || *top.DiscountPercent <= 0 {
		t.Fatalf("the demo catalog must have a discounted product first, got %+v", top)
	}
	if got, want := discountTile(), fmt.Sprintf("Up to −%d%%", *top.DiscountPercent); got != want {
		t.Errorf("discount tile = %q, want %q", got, want)
	}

	// Make the top product's cheapest offer cheaper: its discount grows and the tile follows.
	// The old price is put back when the test ends.
	d, err := e.catalog.GetProduct(ctx, top.Slug)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cheapest := d.Offers[0]
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		if _, err := e.pool.Exec(context.Background(), `UPDATE offers SET price_cents = $2 WHERE id = $1`, cheapest.OfferID, cheapest.PriceCents); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("restore the offer price: %v", err)
		}
	})
	if _, err := e.pool.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE offers SET price_cents = $2 WHERE id = $1`, cheapest.OfferID, cheapest.PriceCents/2); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	after := topOfDiscountListing()
	if after.DiscountPercent == nil || *after.DiscountPercent <= *top.DiscountPercent {
		t.Fatalf("halving the price did not raise the biggest discount: %v -> %v", top.DiscountPercent, after.DiscountPercent)
	}
	if got, want := discountTile(), fmt.Sprintf("Up to −%d%%", *after.DiscountPercent); got != want {
		t.Errorf("after the price change the tile = %q, want %q", got, want)
	}
}

## Gyva didžiausia nuolaida plytelėje (`migrations/00007_homepage_live_discount.sql`, `Service.Tiles`, `maxDiscount`)
- **Ką daro:** plytelės pavadinime žodis `{maxDiscount}` API pakeičiamas didžiausia **dabartine** nuolaida: „Up to −{maxDiscount}%" → „Up to −73%". Skaičius – pirma kortelė Store sąraše, surikiuotame pagal nuolaidą (`ListProducts`, `sort=discount`, 1 elementas), t. y. tas pats sąrašas, į kurį plytelė veda (`/store?sort=discount`). Katalogas paklausiamas vieną kartą per užklausą, kad ir kiek plytelių naudotų žodį.
- **Kodėl taip:** fiksuotas „−70%" pasensta pasikeitus kainoms (peržiūroje: katalogo maksimumas jau 73%). Dabar skaičius visada sutampa su tuo, ką pirkėjas ras paspaudęs. Jei nė vienas perkamas produktas neturi nuolaidos, plytelė praleidžiama – „Up to −0%" būtų nesąmonė (ta pati logika kaip herojaus skaidrė be sandėlio).
- **Kodėl nauja migracija, o ne 00006 pataisymas:** 00006 jau pritaikyta `dev` šakoje (ir galbūt produkcijoje, jei savininkas paleido Migrate production prieš merge). Pritaikytos migracijos niekada neredaguojamos – keičiama nauja migracija (`UPDATE homepage_tiles SET title = ...`), su `Down`, kuris grąžina seną tekstą.
- **Testai:** unit (`service_test.go`) – pakeitimas, vienas katalogo klausimas, plytelė praleidžiama be nuolaidos (tuščias katalogas, nėra MSRP, 0 %, tik išparduoti), katalogo klaida → klaida. Integracinis (`TestHomepageIntegration_discountTileShowsTheLiveMaxDiscount`) – tikroje DB skaičius sutampa su Store sąrašu, o perpus sumažinus kainą plytelė rodo naują, didesnę nuolaidą.
- **Gali paklausti:** „Kodėl ne frontend'e skaičiuojat?" – frontend'as tik piešia, ką gauna; be to, jis turėtų atskirai klausti katalogo. „Ar kešas nesugadina skaičiaus?" – iki 5 min. senumo, kaip ir kainos kortelėse; checkout'as vis tiek skaičiuoja tikrą kainą.
'''
s=s.replace(anchor, anchor+add)
io.open(p,'w',encoding='utf-8',newline='\n').write(s)
EOF
git diff --stat docs/PAAISKINIMAI_LT.md
